Agentic Artificial Intelligence (AI) represents a shift from standalone Large Language Model (LLM) toward closed-loop cognitive architectures that integrate perception, memory, reasoning/planning, and action to enable adaptive, goal-directed swarm behavior. Within this framework, Agentic AI provides a unifying structure for autonomous and adaptive swarm operations while expanding the system attack surface compared to conventional AI systems. This paper proposes LLM-Centric Agentic AI for UAV Swarms (LAUS) and reviews key enabling technologies such as onboard and edge computing, 5G/6G connectivity, multimodal intelligence, and cybersecurity mechanisms, and analyzes threats such as Priority Manipulation Attacks (PMA) that can distort decision-making and degrade network performance. Finally, it identifies open research challenges, including hallucination-resistant reasoning, onboard LLM deployment under SWaP constraints, and standardized security benchmarks for perception-reasoning attacks in agentic UAV systems.

</details>


### [74] [OmniSCS: Omni Safety-Critical Scenario Synthesis for Autonomous Driving via a Fully Editable Driving World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09764)
*Xiaoyun Dong,Qian Xu,Yang Lu,Yang Lou,Yung-Hui Li,Jianping Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniSCS是一个创新系统，用于生成高真实感和物理保真度的安全关键场景，支持闭环测试，在nuScenes、Waymo和KITTI数据集上优于现有方法，并支持实时（13Hz）闭环测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在场景编辑后难以保持数据保真度，且无法高效生成高质量的安全关键场景。

Method: 包含两个核心模块：1）全可编辑驾驶世界构建模块，通过双策略智能体重建和深度精化背景重建保持高保真度；2）安全关键场景合成模块，支持物体插入和轨迹编辑以生成多样化SCS。

Result: 在nuScenes、Waymo和KITTI数据集上编辑场景保真度优于SOTA方法，支持实时（13Hz）闭环测试，并能增强自动驾驶算法。

Conclusion: OmniSCS为自动驾驶中的安全关键场景优化和测试提供了更安全、有效且经济高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The synthesis of safety-critical scenarios (SCS) and their evaluation through closed-loop simulations are crucial for developing robust autonomous driving systems. A key aspect of this process involves editing agent states in both appearance and trajectory levels within existing scenes. However, current methods struggle to preserve data fidelity after scene editing and fail to efficiently generate high-quality SCS through such modifications. To overcome these limitations, we propose OmniSCS, an innovative system that generates photorealistic SCS with high physical fidelity while enabling closed-loop testing in synthetic environments. OmniSCS comprises two key modules: 1) A Fully Editable Driving World Construction module that maintains high-fidelity agent appearance and background during scene editing via dual-strategy agent reconstruction and depth-refinement background reconstruction methods. 2) A SCS Synthesis module that facilitates object insertion and agent trajectory editing to synthesize diverse SCS while preserving data fidelity. Experiments on nuScenes, Waymo, and KITTI datasets show that OmniSCS outperforms state-of-the-art methods in edited scene fidelity. We further validate its ability to enhance autonomous driving algorithms and support real-time (13Hz) closed-loop testing. Overall, OmniSCS provides a safer, more effective, and cost-efficient solution for SCS optimization and testing in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [75] [A Risk-Field Enhanced Closed-Loop Digital Twin Framework for Autonomous Driving Safety Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09772)
*Yongzhi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于风险场增强的闭环数字孪生框架，用于自动驾驶安全验证。该框架集成了物理数据采集、数据同步、虚拟孪生重建、风险感知场景生成、算法评估和安全性分析。通过引入驾驶风险场作为统一中间表示，描述自车周围的障碍物、车道偏离、道路边界、碰撞时间和舒适性相关风险，用于排序高风险场景并为强化学习驾驶策略提供密集的安全引导。实验表明，将显式风险结构嵌入数字孪生体可使自动驾驶验证更具目标性、可解释性和可复用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶安全验证方法存在大规模路测成本高、难以复现、难以暴露罕见危险场景等问题；传统离线仿真虽改善了可重复性，但无法连续连接物理交通状态、虚拟重建、算法评估和场景演化之间的闭环。

Method: 提出风险场增强的闭环数字孪生框架，包含物理数据采集与同步、虚拟孪生重建、风险感知场景生成、自动驾驶算法评估与安全分析。引入驾驶风险场作为统一中间表示，涵盖障碍物风险、车道偏离风险、道路边界风险、碰撞时间风险和舒适性风险，用于高风险场景排序和强化学习策略的密集安全引导。

Result: 提出的风险场引导方法在仿真评估协议下优于传统强化学习基线和风险惩罚基线方法，表明嵌入显式风险结构能够提升验证的目标性、可解释性和可复用性。

Conclusion: 将显式风险结构嵌入数字孪生体可提升自动驾驶验证效果，但其实际有效性仍受限于模型保真度、风险校准和仿真到现实迁移等因素。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems require reliable safety validation before real-world deployment. However, large-scale road testing is costly, difffcult to reproduce, and inefffcient for exposing rare safety-critical scenarios. Conventional simulation improves repeatability, but an offfine simulator alone cannot continuously connect physical trafffc states, virtual reconstruction, algorithm evaluation, and scenario evolution. This paper proposes a risk-ffeld enhanced closed-loop digital twin framework for autonomous driving safety validation. The framework integrates physical data acquisition, data synchronization, virtual twin reconstruction, risk-aware scenario generation, autonomous driving algorithm evaluation, and safety analysis. A driving risk ffeld is introduced as a uniffed intermediate representation to describe obstacle, lane-departure, road-boundary, time-to-collision, and comfort-related risks around the ego vehicle. The risk ffeld ranks high-risk scenarios in the digital twin scenario library and provides dense safety guidance for reinforcement learning-based driving policies. A simulation-style evaluation protocol is designed to compare conventional reinforcement learning baselines, risk-penalty baselines, and the proposed risk-ffeld guided method. The study indicates that embedding explicit risk structure into digital twins can make autonomous driving validation more targeted, interpretable, and reusable, while its practical effectiveness remains bounded by model ffdelity, risk calibration, and sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [76] [Maximizing Human Efficiency in Large-Scale Robot Post-Training via VLAC-Cut Guided Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09776)
*Shaopeng Zhai,Qi Zhang,Tianyi Zhang,Haoran Zhang,Fuxian Huang,Zhanhui Lin,Zijun Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型后训练阶段的人效优化流水线，通过人类角色分工（远程操作员+现场监控员）和自动化轨迹筛选工具（VLAC-CUT），在少量人类监督下实现多机器人协作训练；在四个真实操作任务中，经过迭代后训练策略成功率提升至80%-95%，任务吞吐量提升1.7-4.2倍，且VLAC-CUT在相同人工干预预算下优于纯人机循环训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在下游任务适应中需要多轮后训练迭代，而单轮数据无法解决所有问题；如何最大化单位人力和时间投入下的策略改进与任务吞吐量（即人效）是关键需求。

Method: 提出人效优化后训练流水线：(1) 专业分工——远程操作员负责高价值远程干预与恢复演示，现场监控员负责多机器人监控、接管触发与物理重置；(2) VLAC-CUT工具——自动将机器人轨迹分割为进展、空闲、失败诱导、恢复四类，保留有效片段、过滤有害/无效数据，与人在环数据合并用于下一轮训练。

Result: 在四个真实操作任务上，迭代后训练后最终策略成功率达80%-95%，任务吞吐量相比基础模型提升1.7-4.2倍；在相同人工干预预算下，VLAC-CUT引导的轨迹复用优于纯人机循环训练。

Conclusion: 通过角色专业分工可降低任务切换与操作员培训成本，使有限人力资源能监督更大规模机器人集群；结合自动轨迹筛选可显著提升数据利用率与后训练效率。

Abstract: When adapting Vision Language Action (VLA) models to downstream tasks, multiple rounds of post training are required because a single round of data cannot resolve all issues, making continuous iterations necessary to progressively address the weaknesses exposed in previous rounds. In this report, we aim to maximize human efficiency during post-training, defined as the policy improvement and task throughput achieved per unit of human labor and time.
  We propose a human-efficient post-training pipeline that enables a small number of human operators to supervise multiple robots. The pipeline is built around a specialized division of labor: a trained Teleoperator focuses on high-value remote interventions and recovery demonstrations, while a Floor Operator monitors multiple robots, triggers takeovers, and performs physical resets. This role specialization reduces task switching, lowers operator training costs, and allows limited human labor to supervise more robot interaction across a larger fleet. To improve data utilization efficiency, we introduce VLAC-CUT as an automatic rollout curation tool. It segments autonomous robot trajectories into progress-making, idle, failure-inducing, and recovery portions, preserving useful segments while filtering harmful or uninformative ones. The curated rollout data are combined with Human-in-the-Loop data for the next post-training round. We validate the proposed pipeline on four real-world manipulation tasks. Across iterative post-training rounds, the final policies achieve 80\%--95\% success rates and improve task throughput by 1.7$\times$--4.2$\times$ over the base model. Under the same human-intervention budget, VLAC-CUT guided rollout reuse outperforms HITL-only training in both success rate and throughput.

</details>


### [77] [A Comprehensive Survey and Systematic Real-World Evaluation of Embodied Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09792)
*Liuyi Wang,Kai Sheng,Zongtao He,Jinlong Li,Yongrui Qin,Haojie Dai,Xiangyi Wang,Jingwei Yang,Qingqing Yan,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对视觉与语言导航（VLN）研究进行了全面综述，按照动作范式（层次化 vs. 整体化）和模型范式（判别式 vs. 生成式）对现有方法进行系统分类，并在真实机器人平台上进行了系统性评估，发现仿真与真实部署之间存在显著性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主导航系统大多依赖高度结构化的模型和强先验假设，在开放和不确定的真实环境中鲁棒性有限。VLN通过将自然语言理解与视觉感知相结合提供了一种有前景的替代方案，但缺乏系统的方法论分类和真实世界验证。

Method: 按照两个正交维度对现有方法进行系统分类：动作范式（层次化框架 vs. 整体化框架）和模型范式（判别式方法 vs. 生成式方法）；并在物理机器人平台上对代表性VLN系统配置进行系统性的真实世界评估。

Result: 在10个真实场景的实验中，代表性整体化纯RGB方法在仿真中成功率为61%，但在真实部署中降至22%；而层次化框架在真实世界中的成功率为51%，展现出更强的鲁棒性，揭示了仿真与真实部署之间存在显著的性能差距。

Conclusion: VLN研究在感知、决策和控制方面仍面临关键挑战，需要在未来研究中加以解决。

Abstract: Navigation is a fundamental capability of autonomous systems, yet most existing approaches rely on highly structured models and strong prior assumptions, limiting their robustness in open and uncertain real-world environments. Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) offers a promising direction by enabling robots to integrate natural language understanding with visual perception in a data-driven manner. Although VLN has attracted increasing research attention, systematic methodological taxonomy and real-world validation remain limited. This survey presents a comprehensive review of VLN research. Specifically, state-of-the-art methods are organized along two orthogonal dimensions: action paradigms, including hierarchical and monolithic frameworks, and model paradigms, including discriminative and generative approaches. A critical analysis of their respective strengths and limitations is provided. Additionally, we conduct a systematic real-world evaluation of representative VLN system configurations on a physical robotic platform. Experiments across ten diverse real-world scenes show a substantial performance gap between simulation and real-world deployment under the tested configurations: a representative monolithic RGB-only method achieves 61% success in simulation but drops to 22% in real-world deployment, while a hierarchical framework achieves a higher real-world success rate of 51%, suggesting stronger robustness in our evaluation setting. Finally, we highlight key challenges in perception, decision-making, and control that must be addressed in future research.

</details>


### [78] [A Biomimetic Myoelectric Tentacle Prosthesis with Sensorless Object Detection and Vibrotactile Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09807)
*Gabrielle Marion,Olivier Lecompte,Amandine Gesta,Abolfazl Mohebbi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种肌电触手假肢，结合EMG控制、无传感器物体检测和振动触觉反馈，通过对数螺旋弯曲结构实现自适应抓取，平均响应时间77ms，物体检测成功率超90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种响应迅速、直觉化的辅助设备，能够适应不同物体形状并提供感觉反馈，同时探索超越传统拟人形态的更具表现力的人工肢体设计。

Method: 采用对数螺旋曲线设计的柔性仿生触手结构，通过EMG信号归一化滤波及阈值法识别用户意图；基于电机电流斜率分析实现无传感器物体接触检测；采用累积振动触觉刺激策略传递触手构型空间信息。

Result: 平均响应时间77ms，实现流畅实时交互；物体检测成功率超90%，对EMG变异性具有鲁棒性；用户可可靠识别触手折叠区域。

Conclusion: 该仿生设计证明，优先考虑表达性功能而非遵循预定义拟人形态，是一种有前景的假肢开发方向，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: This paper presents the design and evaluation of a myoelectric tentacle-shaped prosthesis integrating electromyographic (EMG) control, sensorless object detection, and vibrotactile feedback. The objective was to develop a responsive and intuitive assistive device that adapts to various object shapes while providing sensory feedback to the user. The system relies on EMG signals to control the motion of a flexible, biomimetic structure whose curling geometry follows a logarithmic spiral, enabling it to coil around objects. To ensure stable control, the EMG signal is normalized and filtered, and a threshold-based method identifies user intention. Object contact is detected through a slope-based analysis of motor current, eliminating the need for external sensors, and a haptic feedback strategy based on cumulative vibrotactile stimulation conveys spatial information about the tentacle's configuration. The system was evaluated through quantitative and qualitative tests. The results demonstrate a low response time (77 ms on average), enabling smooth real-time interaction; an object-detection success rate above 90%, confirming robustness despite EMG variability; and an effective haptic feedback strategy that allowed users to reliably identify the folding zone of the tentacle. The proposed biomimetic design promotes further investigation of expressive artificial limbs by prioritizing expressive functionality over adherence to a predefined, anthropomorphic form factor.

</details>


### [79] [RASR: Range-Aware Scale Recovery for Metric UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09815)
*Hongtao Liang,Xinyu Shao,Chenxu Wang,Yiyao Wan,Jiahuan Ji,Fangwei Ye,Fuhui Zhou,Qihui Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对无人机在GNSS拒止环境下的末段导航需求，提出Range-Aware Scale Recovery (RASR)方法，通过将可迁移的尺度恢复核心与协议特定的校准模块分离，在PairUAV评估中达到0.003189的总分，有效解决了稠密配对几何基础模型度量输出尺度校准不良的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GNSS拒止环境下，无人机控制器仍需可执行的精确距离和航向指令，但现有稠密配对几何基础模型的度量输出尺度校准不良，仅校正平均尺度仍会在目标附近残留与距离相关的误差。

Method: RASR将可迁移的尺度恢复核心与协议特定的校准模块分离。核心部分压缩冻结的MASt3R风格几何特征为紧凑描述子，利用全局校准恢复主要度量信号；校准模块包含范围桶残差校正和指令网格对齐，以匹配PairUAV的命令格式和评估协议。

Result: 在Multimedia 2026 PairUAV在线评估中，RASR达到0.003189的总分。

Conclusion: 冻结的配对几何可稳定估计每对的距离和航向，而所有协议特定的调整均被限制在推理前固定的校准模块内，实现了尺度恢复的泛化性与协议适配性的解耦。

Abstract: Under Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) denial, a UAV controller still needs a distance and heading command it can execute, making accurate metric last-meter navigation essential. Dense pair-geometry foundation models transfer relative structure well, yet the distance scale of their raw metric outputs remains poorly calibrated. Under the relative error metric of PairUAV, correcting only the average scale can still leave costly, distance-dependent residuals near the goal. To address this scale mismatch, Range-Aware Scale Recovery (RASR) separates a transferable scale-recovery core from a protocol-specific calibration module in a per-pair system fixed at inference. The core compresses frozen Matching And Stereo 3D Reconstruction (MASt3R)-style geometry into a compact descriptor and uses global calibration to recover the dominant metric signal. Range-bucket residual correction and command-grid alignment stay inside the calibration module, so they match the command format and evaluation protocol of PairUAV. On the UAVs in Multimedia 2026 PairUAV online evaluation, RASR reaches a total score of 0.003189. Under the PairUAV protocol, frozen pair geometry thus yields stable per-pair distance and heading estimates, while every protocol-specific adjustment stays confined to a calibration module fixed before inference. Code and materials are available at https://github.com/lht-research/rasr-pairuav.

</details>


### [80] [TS-Mask VLA: 2D Temporal-Spatial Masking for Vision-Language-Action Model with Effective Bridging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09818)
*Shengzhuo Yang,Ronghao Yu,Chuanjie Lv,Linpeng Peng,Hang Yu,Jie Ren,Jiajun Lv,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TS-Mask VLA框架，通过离散扩散动作专家和时空2D掩码策略，在机器人操作任务中以0.5B参数达到95.7%的成功率，优于更大规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自回归token-based动作生成范式将动作简化为下一个token预测，缺乏对动作序列时空结构的显式建模以及视觉-语言表示与动作之间的解耦，限制了模型在长程和复杂场景下的性能。

Method: (1) 离散扩散动作专家（Discrete Diffusion Action Expert）配合桥接注意力条件桥（Bridge Attention conditioning bridge），实现来自VLM的多层条件注入，提升动作生成的准确性和稳定性；(2) 时空2D掩码策略（temporal-spatial 2D masking），增强模型对跨时间依赖和跨维度耦合的理解，生成结构更一致的动作序列。

Result: LIBERO基准上以0.5B参数达到95.7%平均成功率，超越更大模型；CALVIN上达到最佳平均序列长度4.19，长程性能优异。

Conclusion: TS-Mask VLA通过扩散动作生成和时空掩码设计有效提升了VLA模型在机器人操作任务中的性能，验证了该框架的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models aim to understand natural-language instructions and visual observations, and to generate and execute corresponding actions as embodied agents. Recently, autoregressive token-based action generation has driven the development of many representative VLA models. However, this paradigm often reduces action generation to next-token prediction, thereby lacking explicit modeling of the spatiotemporal structure of action sequences and the disentanglement between vision-language representations and actions, which can limit performance in long-horizon and complex scenarios. In this paper, we propose TS-Mask VLA, a vision-language-action framework for robot manipulation. TS-Mask VLA is built upon two key designs: (1) a Discrete Diffusion Action Expert equipped with a Bridge Attention conditioning bridge, which enables multi-layer conditioning from the VLM and facilitates more accurate and stable action generation; and (2) a temporal-spatial 2D masking strategy for discrete action tokens that strengthens the model's understanding of cross-time dependencies and inter-dimensional coupling, leading to more structurally consistent action sequences. We conduct extensive experiments on simulation benchmarks and real-world tasks. On LIBERO, TS-Mask VLA achieves a 95.7 percent average success rate with only 0.5B parameters, outperforming significantly larger models. On CALVIN, it attains the best average sequence length of 4.19 and strong long-horizon performance. Comprehensive analyses and ablations further validate the effectiveness of our design.

</details>


### [81] [More Structure, Not More Capacity: Object-Centric Representations for Visuomotor Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09825)
*Yi Li,Alexandre Chapin,Liming Chen,Jan Peters,Alap Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在ManiSkill3 PickCube-v1任务中，使用冻结的SPOT（DINO ViT-B/16 + Slot Attention）物体中心编码器，相比密集DINO全局特征基线，成功率从32.6%提升至55.0%；加入2D空间目标和原生分辨率渲染后达到68.7%，接近3D oracle上界71.7%。自动运动学失败分类将失败分为近失（空间精度）和无抓取（目标跟踪）两类，空间接地减少近失但对无抓取无效，该分类可迁移至StackCube-v1任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作策略依赖的视觉编码器（全局嵌入或密集补丁网格）混合了任务相关与无关特征。物体中心槽表示（object-centric slot representations）通过将特征分组为每个物体对应的几个槽，提供了一种结构化替代方案。研究旨在检验这种结构在操作任务中能带来多大收益。

Method: 在ManiSkill3 PickCube-v1任务上，控制策略、目标token、渲染和标定不变，仅替换编码器。比较SPOT（DINO ViT-B/16 + Slot Attention）物体中心表示与密集DINO全局特征基线、16倍tokens的密集补丁网格。加入2D空间目标和原生分辨率渲染提升性能。设计自动运动学失败分类法，区分近失（空间精度）和无抓取（目标跟踪）两类失败。

Result: 冻结SPOT编码器成功率达55.0±2.9%，高出密集DINO全局特征基线（32.6±1.5%）22.4%。密集补丁网格（16倍tokens）表现不优于全局特征。加入2D空间目标和原生分辨率渲染后达68.7±4.2%，接近3D oracle上界71.7±4.1%。失败分类显示：空间接地减少近失失败，但不影响无抓取失败。该分类可迁移至StackCube-v1，指出遮挡是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 物体中心槽表示在操作任务中显著优于全局特征和密集补丁网格，且无需微调解码器。空间接地能有效解决空间精度问题，但目标跟踪错误仍需其他机制解决。自动运动学失败分类法可跨任务迁移，为系统瓶颈分析提供工具。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation policies rely on pre-trained vision models that give either a global scene embedding or a dense patch grid. Both mix task-relevant and task-irrelevant features. Object-centric slot representations are a structured alternative: they group features into a few per-object slots. We test what this structure buys on ManiSkill3 PickCube-v1, with a frozen encoder and a held-out-seed evaluation. Holding the policy, goal token, rendering, and calibration fixed and changing only the encoder, a frozen object-centric SPOT representation (DINO ViT-B/16 + Slot Attention) reaches 55.0$\pm$2.9% success, 22.4% above a dense DINO global-feature baseline (32.6 $\pm$ 1.5%), with the same trainable policy and no encoder fine-tuning. More tokens alone do not help: a dense patch grid with 16x the tokens performs no better than the global feature. Adding an explicit 2D spatial goal and native-resolution rendering raises the full system to 68.7$\pm$4.2%, just below a privileged 3D-oracle upper bound (71.7$\pm$4.1%). An automated kinematic failure taxonomy then separates spatial-precision (Near-Miss) failures from object-tracking (No-Grasp) failures: spatial grounding reduces Near-Miss while leaving No- Grasp unchanged. The same taxonomy transfers to the harder StackCube-v1 and points to occlusion as the main bottleneck.

</details>


### [82] [Robo-ValueRL: Reliable Value Estimation for Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09866)
*Wenke Xia,Pei Ren,Wenbo Yu,Yizhuo Zhang,Jifan Li,Yixue Zhang,Yinuo Zhao,Qingyang Gao,Jianlong Fu,Jian Tang,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhengping Che,Di Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Robo-ValueRL框架，通过历史条件值估计与全局进展/局部偏好可靠性度量，系统分析值函数可靠性如何影响离线到在线强化学习的策略预训练与在线改进，在精细芯片插入（86%成功率）和通用积木拆卸（84%成功率）任务中取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线到在线强化学习在全栈复杂性下难以复现和诊断，而值估计在异构数据优先级排序中起核心作用，但其可靠性如何影响策略优化这一关键问题尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出Robo-ValueRL框架：(1) 学习历史条件值估计器；(2) 通过全局进展和局部偏好指标评估值可靠性；(3) 基于可靠性度量的质量条件一致性策略预训练；(4) 在线交互中的残差适应模块。

Result: 在240小时离线演示和3000+在线轨迹实验中，值可靠性与下游性能高度相关；可靠值函数使值引导的离线RL优于行为克隆，并稳定在线改进；系统在精密芯片插入任务达86%成功率，通用积木拆卸任务达84%成功率。

Conclusion: 值函数可靠性对策略性能至关重要，可靠值估计能有效提升异构机器人经验中的数据利用率，为离线到在线强化学习提供实用的统一测试平台。

Abstract: Offline-to-online reinforcement learning is promising for generalizable robotic manipulation, yet its full-stack complexity obscures reproduction and diagnosis. Within such systems, value estimation plays a central role in prioritizing heterogeneous data for policy improvement. Despite its importance, the central question remains underexplored: how value-function reliability shapes policy optimization in offline-to-online reinforcement learning. To answer this question, we propose Robo-ValueRL, a unified framework that enables reliable value estimation and systematically traces its downstream effects on policy pretraining and online improvement. Concretely, Robo-ValueRL learns a history-conditioned value estimator and evaluates its reliability through global-progress and local-preference metrics. These resulting value estimates are propagated into quality-conditioned consistency-policy pretraining and a residual adaptation module on online rollouts, providing a unified testbed for analyzing how value reliability shapes downstream policy performance. Across 240 hours of offline demonstrations and over 3,000 online rollout trajectories, our extensive experiments show that downstream performance is strongly associated with value reliability. Reliable value functions provide better action-quality estimates, allowing value-guided offline RL to scale more effectively than quality-agnostic behavior cloning, and stabilize online improvement by prioritizing high-quality rollout data. Integrating reliable value guidance through offline pretraining with online improvement, our system achieves 86% success on millimeter-level precise chip insertion and 84% on generalizable block disassembly. We hope these findings highlight the importance of value-guided data utilization for effective policy improvement from heterogeneous robotic experience.

</details>


### [83] [Diffusion for Long-Horizon Multi-Robot Path Planning in Human-Shared Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09911)
*Vaibhav Sanjay,Yorai Shaoul,Jiaoyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MRRD是一个结合滚动时域、并行扩散推理、冲突搜索和差异化引导的多机器人路径规划框架，可在密集人群中实现15台机器人的实时、长时域、社交感知导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的生成式规划器受限于固定路径时长和高计算延迟，难以适应不同目标距离和实时部署需求，亟需一种能在人机共存环境中实现实时、长时域、可扩展的多机器人路径规划方法。

Method: 结合滚动时域方案适应人类运动有限预测范围、并行化扩散推理实现类人路径的可扩展生成、基于冲突搜索的机制解决机器人间碰撞、基于紧迫性的时间条件生成变速度路径、差异化引导项最大化社交意识与机器人间协调效率。

Result: 在拥挤环境中成功扩展到15台机器人实时运行，在安全性和任务成功率上显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: MRRD通过滚动扩散与并行化推理，有效克服了固定时长和高延迟限制，实现了大规模机器人团队在密集人群中的实时社交感知导航。

Abstract: Multi-robot path planning in human-shared environments requires a delicate balance between robust inter-robot coordination and socially aware behavior. While diffusion models excel at generating predictable, human-like paths, existing generative planners are often restricted to paths of fixed duration and high computational latency, limiting their adaptability to varying goal distances and hindering real-time deployment. We present Multi-Robot Rolling Diffusion (MRRD), a novel framework that enables real-time, long-horizon navigation for large robot teams through dense crowds. MRRD combines a rolling-horizon scheme to accommodate the limited prediction horizon of human motion, parallelized diffusion inference for scalable generation of human-like paths, and a conflict-based-search mechanism for resolving inter-robot collisions. It further incorporates urgency-based temporal conditioning to generate paths with varying speeds and employs differentiated guidance terms to maximize both social awareness around humans and efficient coordination between robots. Experimental results in crowded environments demonstrate that MRRD successfully scales to 15 robots in real-time, significantly outperforming existing baselines in both safety and mission success rates.

</details>


### [84] [A Numerically-Robust ROS 2 Port of iG-LIO: Diagnosing and Fixing Toolchain-Induced Failures in Incremental GICP LiDAR-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09947)
*Afonso E. Carvalho,David Portugal,Paulo Peixoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文报告了iG-LIO算法从ROS 1到ROS 2 Jazzy的移植过程，重点诊断了移植后出现的由环境引起的数值发散问题（NaN值），根源在于ROS 2工具链中的QoS不匹配（导致IMU数据静默丢弃和乱序）以及oneTBB+Eigen组合中未初始化的并行归约累加器。此外还修正了Ouster点场解析、新增Velodyne Velarray M1600支持、提供Livox传感器双路径支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将iG-LIO从ROS 1移植到ROS 2 Jazzy，并诊断移植后出现的算法层面以外的环境诱导数值故障。

Method: 保持算法数学部分不变，进行机械忠实的代码迁移；通过诊断定位到ROS 2工具链的两个问题：QoS配置不当导致IMU数据异常、oneTBB+Eigen组合的未初始化并行归约累加器。

Result: 成功移植并修复了环境导致的数值发散问题；修正了Ouster点场解析；新增Velodyne Velarray M1600支持；提供Livox CustomMsg和标准PointCloud2双路径；在Ouster OS0 Rev7、Ouster OS1 Rev7、Livox MID-360上完成验证。

Conclusion: 本报告作为移植工作的可引用参考文献，而非对底层算法的新贡献。

Abstract: iG-LIO is a tightly-coupled LiDAR-inertial odometry system fusing generalized-ICP and point-to-plane constraints in an iterated error-state Kalman filter over an incremental voxel map. We report an open-source ROS 2 Jazzy port of the original ROS 1 implementation and, more importantly, the diagnosis of environment-induced numerical failures that appear only after the port: a mechanically faithful migration -- estimation mathematics left unchanged -- compiled and ran, yet diverged with NaN internal values. Both causes trace to the modern ROS 2 toolchain, not the algorithm: a Quality-of-Service (QoS) mismatch that silently drops and reorders IMU samples, and an uninitialized parallel-reduce accumulator arising from the oneTBB + Eigen combination shipped with current distributions. We further correct Ouster point-field parsing to ensure correct point cloud undistortion with newer Ouster revisions, add Velodyne Velarray M1600 support, provide both a compile-time-gated Livox CustomMsg path and a driver-free path for Livox sensors publishing standard PointCloud2 (e.g. Mid-360), and expose the runtime via YAML. The result has been validated in an Ouster OS0 Rev7, an Ouster OS1 Rev 7, and a Livox MID-360. This report is a citable reference for the port itself, not a claim on the underlying algorithm [1]. The ROS 2 port of iG-LIO described in this document can be found at https://github.com/Forestry-Robotics-UC/ig_lio/tree/ros2-jazzy.

</details>


### [85] [SEAMLiS: Visibility-Aware Safety for Perception-Limited Multi-Robot Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09959)
*Taekyung Kim,Rahul H Kumar,Aswin D. Menon,Tzu-Hsiang Lin,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SEAMLiS，一种用于多机器人自主探索的安全执行层框架，通过感知感知的姿态和位置滤波器解决有限传感范围下探索规划器过于乐观导致的安全问题，并在仿真和实物实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限传感范围和视场角下，传统探索框架将全局规划与局部安全控制分离，导致探索规划器可能过于乐观地规划通过未观测空间，且传感器朝向信息增益而非运动方向，使得隐藏障碍物被过晚检测，无法在有限驱动下及时避障，存在安全隐患。

Method: SEAMLiS采用模块化执行层安全框架：1）基于门控机制的姿态滤波器，在可见性促进偏航策略与速度跟踪备份策略之间切换，以保持对关键已知自由/未知边界的可见性并保留足够制动余量；2）基于控制障碍函数（CBF）的位置滤波器，用于避开已知障碍物、新检测障碍物及其他机器人。该方法保留了上游探索栈（目标分配器和局部规划器）不变。

Result: 在随机仿真、Isaac Sim和Crazyflie硬件实验中，SEAMLiS在单机器人和多机器人设置下均实现了无碰撞探索，同时保持了可见性促进偏航控制的大部分效率。

Conclusion: 提供了充分的碰撞避免条件，验证了该框架在有限传感条件下保证多机器人探索安全性的有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration in unknown environments is typically driven by informative frontiers, viewpoints, or trajectories, while local safety controllers avoid obstacles represented in the current map. Under finite sensing range and limited field of view, this separation can be unsafe: an exploration stack may plan optimistically through unobserved space and steer the sensor toward information gain rather than along the direction of motion, causing hidden obstacles to be detected too late for bounded-actuation avoidance. This paper presents SEAMLiS (Safe Exploration for Autonomous Multi-Robot Systems Under Limited Sensing), a modular execution-layer safety framework for decentralized multi-robot exploration. SEAMLiS preserves the upstream exploration stack, including the goal allocator and local planner, and enforces safety at the execution layer through perception-aware attitude and positional filters. A gatekeeper-based attitude filter switches between a visibility-promoting yaw policy and a velocity-tracking backup policy to preserve visibility of the critical known-free/unknown boundary with sufficient braking margin. A Control Barrier Function (CBF)-based positional filter then avoids known obstacles, newly detected obstacles, and other robots. We provide sufficient collision-avoidance conditions and validate the framework in randomized simulation, Isaac Sim, and Crazyflie hardware experiments. Results show collision-free exploration across tested single- and multi-robot settings while retaining much of the efficiency of visibility-promoting yaw control.

</details>


### [86] [Task Planning for Mobile Manipulation in Retail Stores using Foundation Models with Iterative Re-planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09962)
*Vismay Vakharia,Sanjana Garai,Rolif Lima,Nijil George,Vighnesh Vatsal,Kaushik Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用大语言模型(LLM)和视觉语言模型(VLM)的任务规划方法，用于解决超市等零售场景中的货架补货问题，并在全向移动操作平台上通过用户驱动的提示和基于反馈的迭代重规划实现了端到端系统，在PyBullet仿真环境中进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售、仓储和物流行业的自动化可以提高吞吐量、降低成本并缓解劳动力短缺带来的影响。此前自动化主要集中在结构化环境中的后台操作，而随着机器人移动操作硬件和基础模型的进步，自动化现在可以应用于更具变化性和以人为中心的零售货架环境。

Method: 采用LLM和VLM进行任务规划，构建了自定义全向移动操作平台，使用用户驱动的提示和基于反馈的迭代重规划方法进行错误校正，在PyBullet仿真环境中验证拾取-放置任务。

Result: 在PyBullet仿真环境中成功验证了端到端系统在拾取-放置任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 结合LLM和VLM的任务规划方法能够有效解决零售场景中的补货问题，为机器人在非结构化、人机共存环境中的应用提供了可行的自动化方案。

Abstract: Automation in industries such as retail, warehousing and logistics presents opportunities for greater throughput, cost reduction and mitigation of disruptions from labour shortages. Previously, such efforts have focused on back-room operations involving packing and sorting in relatively structured environments. With advances in robotic mobile manipulation hardware and foundation models, automation can now be applied to more variable and human-centric environments such as retail store shelves. In this work, we present a task-planning approach using Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to address the restocking problem in retail scenarios such as supermarkets. We demonstrate this system on a custom omnidirectional mobile manipulation platform, with user-driven prompts and a feedback-based iterative re-planning approach for error correction. The end-to-end system is validated in a PyBullet simulation environment for pick-and-place tasks.

</details>


### [87] [Chalito: An Extensible Library for Filtering-Based State Estimation in Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09968)
*Hilton Marques Souza Santana,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Marco Antonio Meggiolaro,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Chalito，一个用于四足机器人状态估计算法基准测试的MATLAB/Python开源库。它支持从URDF导入机器人模型、多种滤波方法，可在仿真和真实数据集上运行，旨在解决现有方法难以公平比较和复现的问题。这是首个专门针对四足机器人滤波算法基准测试的开源库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人状态估计方法往往与特定机器人或软件框架紧密耦合，缺乏通用的基准测试框架，导致算法难以公平比较、复现困难，阻碍了算法创新。

Method: 开发了Chalito库，该库可扩展、支持MATLAB/Python，直接导入URDF机器人模型，支持多种滤波方法，并能在仿真和真实数据集上系统性评估不同机器人和滤波器。

Result: 成功实现了一个专门针对四足机器人滤波算法基准测试的开源库，支持多机器人、多滤波器的系统性评估。

Conclusion: Chalito是首个专门用于四足机器人滤波算法基准测试的开源库，填补了该领域缺乏通用基准测试框架的空白，有望提升算法的可复现性和创新速度。

Abstract: State estimation is essential for quadruped robots, enabling robust locomotion, navigation, and control. While many estimators have been proposed in the literature, existing implementations are often tied to specific robots or software stacks, making fair comparisons difficult. This lack of a general-purpose benchmarking framework hinders reproducibility and slows down algorithmic innovation. In this paper, we introduce Chalito, an extensible MATLAB/Python library for benchmarking filter-based state estimation algorithms in quadruped robots. Chalito imports robot models directly from URDF, supports multiple filtering approaches, and is designed to be easily extended with new methods. The framework runs on both simulated and real datasets, enabling systematic evaluation across robots and filters. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first open-source library exclusively dedicated to benchmarking filtering algorithms for quadruped robots.

</details>


### [88] [PinFT: Miniature 5-Axis Force/Torque Sensor Embeddable to Tweezer-like Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10000)
*Leo King,Jenny Chen,Tae Myung Huh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于镊子类工具的微型五轴电容式力/扭矩传感器PinFT，通过紧凑的三PCB堆叠结构和2mm不锈钢销钉实现五自由度力/扭矩传感。实验表明，该传感器在亚毫米级操作任务中能够区分成功操作与失败事件（如滑移和物体弹出），使标准平行夹爪能够监测和解读通过手持镊子执行的精细操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有力传感器难以集成到镊子尖端以实现指尖级的力感知，而精细操作任务（如夹持微小元件、拔毛、撕扯柔性材料）需要直接在操作点进行多轴力/扭矩传感才能准确捕捉操作特征。

Method: 采用三PCB堆叠结构，利用镀通孔分段电极和硅弹性体介电层，通过2mm直径不锈钢销钉的位移实现五自由度（Fx, Fy, Fz, Tx, Ty）电容式力/扭矩传感。使用高阶多项式映射进行校准，并将传感器集成到3D打印镊子中，安装于平行夹爪上进行操作评估。

Result: 校准后力平均绝对误差约0.23N，扭矩平均绝对误差约2.5mN·m，各轴R²均超过0.97。在三种操作任务中，每尖端力传感可靠捕捉了特征力信号，基于内部抓取力和净相互作用力的梯度特征成功区分了成功操作与失败事件。

Conclusion: 直接每尖端力传感使标准平行夹爪能够监测和解读通过手持镊子执行的精细操作任务，实现对滑移和物体弹出等事件的可靠判别。

Abstract: We present PinFT, a miniature five-axis capacitive force/torque sensor designed for direct tip-level integration into tweezer-like tools. The sensor employs a compact three-PCB stack with segmented plated through-hole electrodes and a silicone elastomer dielectric, enabling five-degree-of-freedom force and torque sensing ($F_x$, $F_y$, $F_z$, $T_x$, $T_y$) through displacement of a central 2\,mm-diameter stainless steel pin. The fabricated prototype was calibrated using a higher-order polynomial mapping, yielding mean absolute errors of approximately 0.23\,N for forces and 2.5\,mN$\cdot$m for torques, with coefficients of determination ($R^2$) exceeding 0.97 across all axes. To demonstrate practical utility, a 3D-printed tweezer integrating PinFT sensors at both tips was mounted on a parallel-jaw gripper and evaluated across three representative manipulation tasks: grasping a sub-millimeter SMD capacitor, pulling a simulated hair from a silicone substrate, and tearing a compliant silicone specimen. In all cases, per-tip force sensing reliably captured characteristic force signatures that distinguish successful manipulation from failure events -- including slip and object ejection -- using gradient-based features derived from internal grasp force and net interaction force. These results demonstrate that direct, per-tip force sensing enables standard parallel-jaw grippers to monitor and interpret fine manipulation tasks performed through a handheld tweezer.

</details>


### [89] [Runtime Safety Filtering for Learned Small UAS Separation Policies under GNSS Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10014)
*Alex Zongo,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在GNSS退化环境下，针对小型无人机系统（sUAS）的基于学习的安全间隔策略，比较了动作过滤（通过离散时间控制屏障函数约束策略输出）和观测过滤（将最坏情况状态直接输入策略）两种运行时安全机制。实验表明，观测过滤能将近距空中碰撞减少90%，而动作过滤几乎无安全提升，说明保留策略决策权优于手工约束覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型sUAS间隔策略假设GNSS提供精确位置和速度信息，但在城市环境中多径传播、信号遮挡和有意干扰会降低导航完整性。因此需要研究在GNSS退化条件下，运行时安全机制应如何部署——是过滤策略的动作还是观测。

Method: 两种架构均先估计与有界观测不确定性一致的最坏情况交通状态，然后分叉：动作过滤使用离散时间控制屏障函数在最坏情况状态下约束策略输出；观测过滤则将最坏情况状态作为校正输入直接送入策略。

Result: 动作过滤几乎无安全提升，而观测过滤将近距空中碰撞减少90%，且对屏障函数在分离距离与接近速率之间的权衡具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 对于具有学习型安全行为的策略，保留策略的决策权优于用手工设计的约束覆盖其动作。

Abstract: Learning-based separation assurance for small Unmanned Aircraft Systems (sUAS) achieves near-zero collision rates in simulation, but assumes accurate position and velocity information from Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS). This assumption fails in urban environments, where multipath propagation, signal blockage, and intentional interference degrade navigation integrity. This raises a fundamental architectural question for deploying learned separation policies under GNSS degradation: should runtime safety mechanisms filter the policy's actions or its observations? This work evaluates both approaches for multi-agent sUAS separation under adversarial GNSS degradation. Both architectures first estimate a worst-case traffic state consistent with bounded observation uncertainty, then diverge: action filtering constrains policy outputs via discrete-time control barrier functions evaluated at the worst-case state, while observation filtering presents the worst-case state directly to the policy as corrected input. Experimental results show that action filtering provides negligible safety improvement, while observation filtering reduces near mid-air collisions by 90% and remains robust to the barrier function's tradeoff between separation distance and closing rate. These results suggest that, for policies with learned safety behaviors, preserving the policy's decision authority outperforms overriding its actions with hand-designed constraints.

</details>


### [90] [From Non-Rigid to Rigid: Safe Acquisition of Rigid Communication Graphs under Limited Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10170)
*Saharsh,Vedhas Talnikar,Pushpak Jagtap*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需初始刚性图、在有限感知范围下实现异构非线性多机器人系统无碰撞刚性时变通信图的方法，并设计了基于分布式二次优化的领导者-追随者控制器，仅需局部相对测量即可保证图刚性，通过仿真和实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人编队控制中，通信图刚性是基本要求，但实际中由于机器人感知范围有限和动态环境，确保并维持刚性通信拓扑具有挑战性。现有方法通常假设初始刚性图或需要全局绝对位置，这在实践中难以满足。

Method: 提出一种分布式二次优化控制器，采用领导者-追随者架构，基于层次化二阶一致性实现刚性图获取。无需假设初始刚性图，通信链路根据有限感知范围动态建立/断开，仅需局部相对测量（无需全局绝对位置），适用于异构非线性多机器人系统。

Result: 通过仿真和在运动捕捉环境中的硬件实验验证了该方法在有限感知能力下的可靠性能，实现了无碰撞的刚性时变通信图。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了有限感知范围和动态条件下多机器人系统刚性通信图的构建与维持问题，放宽了对初始条件和全局信息的依赖，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Communication graph rigidity is a fundamental requirement in many multi robot formation control approaches. However, ensuring and maintaining a rigid communication topology becomes challenging in practice due to limited sensing ranges and dynamic operating conditions. This paper provides a method for achieving an inter robot collision free, rigid time varying communication graph, where communication links are established or broken according to limited sensing ranges, without assuming an initial rigid graph. In addition, the proposed approach guarantees the realization of a rigid graph for heterogeneous nonlinear multi robot systems. A computationally lean, distributed quadratic optimization-based controller is developed for a leader follower architecture, acquiring rigidity based on hierarchical second-order consensus among robots. Follower agents do not require global absolute positions of any agent, including their own. The proposed method is validated through both simulations and hardware experiments in a motion-capture environment, demonstrating reliable performance under the limited sensing capabilities of individual robots.

</details>


### [91] [Plug-and-Play Reweighting for Resilient Collaborative Decision-Making in Connected Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10037)
*Jiewen Liu,Rui Liu,Matthew Lee,Ming C. Lin,Xiaorui Liu,Peng Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RCDM框架，通过注意力编码器提取个体感知嵌入、注意力解码器融合协作感知，并设计即插即用的重加权模块，通过分析邻域点与局部结构的一致性降低被破坏输入的影响，无需额外训练即可提升多机器人系统在感知噪声和攻击下的决策鲁棒性，在仿真中实现高达26%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作决策方法依赖特定攻击的防御重训练或对扰动做出严格假设，实用性强，亟需一种不依赖特定攻击假设、无需额外训练的鲁棒协作决策方法。

Method: 提出RCDM框架：(1) 基于注意力的编码器提取单个机器人感知嵌入；(2) 基于注意力的解码器融合协作感知并做出决策；(3) 设计即插即用的重加权模块，通过分析邻域点相对于局部结构的一致性，对偏离局部中位数的点赋予较小权重，从而降低被破坏输入的影响，无需额外训练即可集成到基于注意力的协作决策中。

Result: 在高保真仿真中，考虑感知噪声和五种攻击类型的多样事故场景下，RCDM一致优于现有方法，最高提升26%，达到最先进的鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的RCDM框架通过即插即用的重加权机制有效提升了多机器人协作决策对感知噪声和各类攻击的鲁棒性，无需重新训练，具有良好的实用性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Collaborative decision-making is a fundamental capability in multi-robot systems, such as connected autonomous vehicles. However, perceptual noise and adversarial attacks in collaborators can severely affect decision reliability. Overall, existing methods typically rely on retraining with attack-specific defenses or on restrictive perturbation assumptions to improve resilience, which limits their practicality. In this paper, we propose a novel Resilient Collaborative Decision-Making (RCDM) framework that consists of an attention-based encoder for extracting individual robot perceptual embeddings and an attention-based decoder for fusing collaborator perceptions and making decisions. To improve resilience to corrupted observations, we design a novel plug-and-play reweighting module that down-weights the influence of corrupted inputs by analyzing the consistency of neighborhood points relative to the local structure and assigning smaller weights to points that deviate strongly from the local median. This module can be seamlessly integrated into attention-based collaborative decision-making without requiring additional training. We evaluate our method in high-fidelity simulations, considering perceptual noise and five types of attacks across diverse accident-prone scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms existing methods by up to 26% and achieves state-of-the-art resilient performance.

</details>


### [92] [Diffusion-Residual Model Predictive Steering Control for Vehicle Stabilization at the Limit of Handling under Model Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10243)
*Bongsob Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出扩散残差模型预测控制（D-res），通过条件扩散残差模型在线学习MPC中未知的横摆角参考和稳定操纵包络，离线制表后在线仅需查表即可在100Hz内运行，在多种工况下显著降低峰值侧滑并恢复低附路面的方向稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极限操纵下的MPC依赖未知的先验横摆角参考和稳定操纵包络，固定或最坏情况设置要么过于保守要么不安全，现有方法缺乏对这类不确定性的自适应处理。

Method: 构建条件扩散残差模型，以转向命令为条件输出残差的均值和预测散布；均值用于调整横摆角参考，散布通过单侧机会约束在预测时域内收紧稳定操纵包络，形成D-res控制器。模型离线制表，在线仅需查表，无在环扩散过程。

Result: 在7自由度模型和高保真CarMaker联合仿真中，D-res在固定自行车模型最不准确的区域显著降低峰值侧滑，在低附着力工况下恢复方向稳定性，最坏情况每步4.08ms，满足100Hz实时预算。

Conclusion: 通过扩散残差模型将不确定性预测融入参考和约束而非控制律，D-res实现了超前预警而非滞后高增益校正，兼顾了安全性与实时性。

Abstract: At the limit of handling, a stabilizing MPC depends on the yaw-rate reference it tracks and the stable-handling envelope it enforces, both operating-point-dependent and unknown a priori, so fixed or worst-case settings are either too conservative or unsafe. We learn this uncertainty with a conditional diffusion residual model and apply it to the controller's reference and constraints rather than its control law. Conditioned on the steering command, the model returns the residual's mean and a predictive spread: the mean re-sizes the tracked yaw reference, while the spread, propagated over the prediction horizon, tightens the stable-handling envelope through a one-sided chance back-off. Together these form the proposed diffusion-residual MPC (D-res), so caution is anticipated ahead of the tracking error rather than corrected after it by a high-gain loop. Because only two moments per command are needed, the generator is tabulated offline and the online controller adds a single table lookup to the baseline MPC, with no in-loop diffusion; it runs within the 100 Hz budget on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier (worst-case 4.08 ms per step). Across a 7-DOF model and high-fidelity CarMaker co-simulation spanning vehicle, tire, road, and maneuver diversity, D-res reduces peak side-slip where the fixed bicycle model is least accurate and restores directional stability on low-friction maneuvers, where the fixed reference over-commands the available grip.

</details>


### [93] [Direct Rotor Thrust Sensing and Feedback Control for Disturbance Rejection of Multirotors Using Load-cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10099)
*Peter Böhm,Michael Brünig,Peyman Z. Moghadam,Pauline E. I. Pounds*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过载荷传感器直接测量旋翼瞬时力并实施高速控制的新方法，以快速对抗多旋翼飞行器遭遇的阵风、动态垂直 inflow 和地面效应等复杂气动干扰。实验表明，即使在低成本硬件上，该方法在单/双旋翼跷跷板模型和实际飞行器中均有效可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多旋翼飞行器依赖简化的扰动模型和基于飞行轨迹的间接补偿，导致干扰感知和响应存在延迟。尽管过去15-20年已有诸多快速扰动抑制方法，但仍有挑战。本文旨在通过直接测量旋翼瞬时力来更快速地跟踪期望推力，从而克服传统方法的局限性。

Method: 在旋翼产生推力的位置直接安装载荷传感器（load-cells），实时测量瞬时力信号，并实施高速控制以精确跟踪期望推力。利用低成本商用组件搭建专用硬件，在单旋翼和双旋翼跷跷板模型以及实际飞行器上进行实验验证。

Result: 先前认为载荷传感器噪声过大、无法提供有效输入，但实验结果表明，该方法在复杂气动现象（阵风、动态垂直 inflow、地面效应）存在时，不仅可行而且有效。

Conclusion: 直接力测量结合高速控制能够有效对抗复杂气动干扰，为多旋翼飞行器的扰动抑制提供了新的可行途径，且可基于低成本硬件实现。

Abstract: Gust disturbances, dynamic vertical inflow and ground effect are key adverse aerodynamic phenomena that induce variations in the forces acting on a multirotor and complicate its flight control. Miniature rotorcraft typically rely on simplified modelling of such effects to compute adjustments in thrust to counteract these forces. In the most basic case, disturbance force estimations are derived from the aircraft's motion and the generated thrust is assumed to exactly match that requested by the controller. However, such systems rely on the aircraft's trajectory to be affected before disturbances can be sensed and compensated. Numerous approaches presented over the last 15-20 years aim to reject external disturbances more quickly, but challenges remain.
  This paper presents a new approach in this category by measuring the instantaneous force of the rotors directly at the point of generation using load-cells and implementing high-speed control to accurately track the desired thrust. Measurements from load-cells were previously considered too noisy to provide meaningful input, but the experiments presented in the paper using purpose-built hardware from low-cost commodity components in single- and dual rotor see-saw models and a flying aircraft demonstrate both the feasibility and the effectiveness of the approach in the presence of complex aerodynamic phenomena.

</details>


### [94] [Underwater Dead Reckoning with Deployable Situation-Triggered Covariance Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10597)
*Akshay Naik,Ramavarapu S. Sreenivas,Dustin Nottage,Ahmet Soylemezoglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种情境触发式自适应鲁棒扩展卡尔曼滤波器（Situation-Triggered Calibrated Adaptive Robust Extended Kalman Filter），用于BlueROV2水下航行器的航位推算。该方法通过概率触发器识别运动情境，并切换预标定的噪声矩阵，无需重置滤波器状态。实验表明，相比固定参数滤波器，该方法在无视觉条件下实现了适度且一致的航位推算精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下航位推算在视觉不可用且无法依赖外部定位时至关重要，但固定滤波器参数在转弯、运动过渡或传感器测量可靠性下降时表现不佳，需要一种能自适应调整滤波器参数的方法。

Method: 提出Situation-Triggered Calibrated Adaptive Robust Extended Kalman Filter：1）板载概率触发器识别当前运动情境；2）误差状态滤波器连续运行；3）触发器置信时，仅切换预标定的过程噪声和测量噪声矩阵，不重置状态估计、协方差历史、动力学及测量模型；4）利用AprilTag监督的稀疏池运行离线标定触发器、噪声轮廓及多普勒测速仪偏航对准校正。

Result: 在四次留出池运行测试中，该方法相比全局噪声轮廓的相同滤波器骨干，将标签加权平均每次运行的平移RMSE从0.488m降至0.471m；所有留出运行均偏向调度方法；10秒段的配对bootstrap给出-0.017m的差异，95%置信区间为[-0.024, -0.008]m，方向误差基本不变。

Conclusion: 情境感知的协方差调度无需切换估计器或重置滤波器，即可在无视觉航位推算中实现适度但一致的性能提升，验证了该方法的有效性和可靠性。

Abstract: Underwater dead reckoning estimates vehicle position when vision is unavailable and external positioning cannot be assumed. A single set of filter parameters can work well in many situations, but fixed tuning may be poorly matched during turns, motion transitions, or periods when sensor measurements are less reliable. This paper presents the Situation-Triggered Calibrated Adaptive Robust Extended Kalman Filter for a BlueROV2. An onboard probabilistic trigger identifies the current motion situation while one error-state filter runs continuously. When the trigger is confident, the filter changes only to the corresponding pre-calibrated process- and measurement-noise matrices; the state estimate, covariance history, dynamics, and measurement models are not reset or replaced. The trigger, noise profiles, and a one-time Doppler velocity log yaw-alignment correction are calibrated offline using sparse AprilTag-supervised pool runs. A separate validation set selects the scheduling policy, which is then fixed before held-out testing. Across four held-out pool runs, the method reduces label-weighted mean per-run translation root-mean-square error from 0.488 m to 0.471 m relative to the same filter backbone with one global noise profile, and every held-out run favors the scheduled method. A paired bootstrap over 10-second segments gives a candidate-minus-baseline difference of -0.017 m with a 95% confidence interval of [-0.024, -0.008] m, while orientation error remains essentially unchanged. These results indicate that situation-aware covariance scheduling provides a modest but consistent vision-free dead-reckoning improvement without switching estimators or resetting the filter.

</details>


### [95] [TAC-LOCO: Unified Whole-Body Control for Quadrupedal TACtile-Informed LOCO-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10132)
*Muqun Hu,Yuhao Zhou,Kabir Ray Malik,Chi Lin,Won Suk Lee,Yu She,Yan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TAC-LOCO框架，利用触觉传感增强四足机器人全身动态操控，通过强化学习实现抓取力调节和物体防滑，在真实机器人上实现47%抓取力降低和<1%掉落率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有腿部机器人操控工作缺乏触觉反馈，通常采用固定抓取策略而无法根据交互动态调节抓取力，导致在不确定外力下易滑落或抓取力过大。

Method: 提出TAC-LOCO统一强化学习框架，将柔性夹爪的触觉阵列观测编码为紧凑潜变量，与本体感知融合实现对腿、臂、夹爪的统一控制；设计抓取稳定性奖励引导策略同时跟踪身体速度与末端轨迹、调节抓取力并防止滑落。

Result: 在Unitree Go2+Interbotix WidowX 250+触觉夹爪上零样本部署，实现47%抓取力降低，物体掉落率低于1%，适应渐变负载变化和突然释放事件。

Conclusion: 触觉感知对动态全身操控至关重要，所提框架有效协调了抓取力与运动控制的平衡，显著优于无触觉基线方法。

Abstract: Dynamic loco-manipulation requires legged robots to coordinate whole-body motion while maintaining stable physical interaction with grasped objects under uncertain external forces. While tactile sensing has been widely studied for robotic manipulation, its role in dynamic whole-body control remains largely unexplored. Existing works without tactile feedback commonly grasp firmly rather than regulate the grasp according to the interaction. We propose TAC-LOCO, a tactile-augmented unified reinforcement learning framework that encodes tactile array observations from compliant grippers into a compact latent representation and joins it with proprioception for unified control of the legs, arm, and gripper. With effective grasp stability reward design, the policy learns to simultaneously track body velocity and end-effector trajectories, moderate grasp force, and prevent object slip under both gradual load changes and sudden release events. We deploy the policy zero-shot on a Unitree Go2 with an Interbotix WidowX 250 arm and tactile gripper, demonstrating dynamic tactile-informed loco-manipulation under varying external interactions, achieving a 47% reduction in grasping force and an object drop rate of less than 1%.

</details>


### [96] [Millimeter Wave Radar: From Synthetic Aperture to Probabilistic Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10161)
*Jui-Te Huang,Ruoyang Xu,Michael Kaess*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于毫米波雷达的鲁棒概率建图管道，通过合成孔径雷达（SAR）处理和概率建模，在烟雾、雾等恶劣环境下实现自主机器人的可靠建图。实验验证了该方法在室内环境中的有效性，并分析了关键参数和天线阵列配置对建图性能的影响。开源了级联毫米波雷达数据集和GPU加速信号处理管道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统传感器在烟雾和雾等恶劣条件下失效，而毫米波雷达虽能在这些条件下提供可靠感知，但其固有的稀疏性和噪声特性导致难以生成准确的概率地图。因此，需要建立从原始雷达信号到概率占用地图的完整管道。

Method: 建立从原始雷达信号到概率占用地图的完整管道，包括：合成孔径雷达（SAR）信号处理、概率建模步骤，并通过下游路径规划性能评估建图质量。

Result: 实验证明SAR-based概率建图在真实机器人部署中的有效性和局限性，并分析了关键参数和天线阵列配置的影响。

Conclusion: 毫米波雷达结合SAR处理和概率建模可以实现恶劣环境下的鲁棒建图，但仍存在局限性。开源数据集和GPU加速管道可促进该领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: Robust probabilistic mapping is essential for autonomous robotic systems operating in challenging environments. While traditional sensors fail in adverse conditions such as smoke and fog, millimeter wave (mmWave) radar sensors offer reliable sensing in such conditions. However, creating accurate probabilistic maps from radar data presents significant challenges due to the inherently sparse and noisy characteristics of radio wave measurements and signal processing steps. In an attempt to address these issues, we establish a complete pipeline from raw radar signals to probabilistic occupancy maps, incorporating Synthetic Aperture Radar processing followed by a probabilistic modeling step. We conduct extensive validation across indoor environments, comparing our approach against different signal processing and probabilistic modeling approaches. We also evaluate mapping quality through downstream path planning performance analysis. Furthermore, we investigate the impact of key parameters and antenna array configuration on mapping performance. The experimental results demonstrate both the effectiveness and limitations of SAR-based probabilistic mapping for real-world robotic deployment. To facilitate future research and broader adoption, we contribute an open-source cascaded mmWave radar dataset with an accompanying GPU-accelerated signal processing pipeline available at https://github.com/rpl-cmu/rpm.

</details>


### [97] [D-SafeMPC: Diffusion-Driven Safe Model Predictive Control with Discrete-Time Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10842)
*Erdi Sayar,Ersin Daş,Joel W. Burdick,Alois Knoll,Erdal Kayacan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出D-SafeMPC方法，通过将控制屏障函数(CBF)和控制Lyapunov函数(CLF)集成到扩散模型的逆向去噪过程中，并结合迭代投影策略的模型预测控制(MPC)，有效解决了扩散模型在机器人规划中无法保证安全性和动力学约束的问题。在Franka机械臂的仿真和实物实验中，该方法在安全性、任务成功率和规划效率上均优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在机器人规划中的应用受限于其无法固有地保证安全性或动力学约束，常常产生物理上不可行或不安全的输出。现有的混合方法虽采用MPC解决此问题，但因扩散模型提供的较差轨迹初始化导致MPC无法收敛到安全可行的解，存在不稳定性问题。

Method: 提出D-SafeMPC框架，核心创新包括：(1) 在逆向扩散过程中引入控制屏障函数(CBF)和控制Lyapunov函数(CLF)引导去噪过程朝向安全且目标导向的区域；(2) 采用迭代投影方案，在每个去噪步骤中结合MPC对轨迹进行精细化优化，为MPC提供可靠的暖启动。

Result: 在Franka机械臂的四个仿真场景（一个静态障碍物和三个动态障碍物设置）以及从仿真到实际的实物实验中，D-SafeMPC在安全性、任务成功率和规划效率方面均优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: D-SafeMPC通过增强扩散模型与控制之间的交互，有效解决了扩散模型在机器人规划中的安全与约束问题，提升了规划的安全性与可靠性。代码和实验配置已开源。

Abstract: A key limitation on the use of diffusion models in robotic planning is their inability to inherently enforce safety or dynamical constraints, which often results in physically infeasible or unsafe outputs. Hybrid approaches that employ model predictive control (MPC) to address this problem can be unstable, as poor trajectory initializations from the diffusion model prevent the MPC from converging to a safe and feasible solution. To overcome these challenges, we propose D-SafeMPC, which enhances the interaction between diffusion and control. Our method guides the reverse diffusion process with control barrier functions (CBFs) and control Lyapunov functions (CLFs) and employs an iterative-projection scheme where an MPC refines the trajectory at each denoising step. This steers sampling toward safe, goal-directed regions and provides reliable MPC warm starts. In simulations on a Franka manipulator across four scenarios (one static-obstacle and three dynamic-obstacle settings) and in a sim-to-real experiment on a physical Franka robot, D-SafeMPC improves safety, task success rates, and planning efficiency over state-of-the-art baselines. To facilitate reproducibility, our source code and experimental configurations are available in a repository at https://github.com/erdiphd/D-SafeMPC

</details>


### [98] [On the Efficiency of LoRA Fine-Tuning for Vision-Language-Action Models in Industrial Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10172)
*Finn Ferchau,Daniel Pommer,Cristian Axenie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LoRA微调方法在π₀流匹配VLA模型上的应用，发现LoRA r=32且全视觉编码器微调即可达到与全微调相当的性能，同时将峰值VRAM从36.2降至10.8 GiB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业硬件上部署十亿级VLA模型需要微调以弥合具身差距，但全微调需要数据中心级GPU资源，成本高昂。因此需要探索更高效的参数高效微调方法。

Method: 以π₀流匹配VLA模型为基础，在UR5e机械臂的四个精密装配任务上进行系统实验，扫描LoRA秩（r=8到256）、分配策略和组件冻结消融，并与全微调进行对比。

Result: LoRA r=32且均匀分配至VLM骨干和动作专家即可达到与全微调无统计显著差异的性能；冻结VLM或限制视觉编码器仅用LoRA会显著降低性能；峰值VRAM从36.2降至10.8 GiB。

Conclusion: LoRA r=32配合全视觉编码器微调是一种实用的工业部署方案，能在保证性能的同时大幅降低显存需求，且具身适配需要语义和视觉双重可塑性。

Abstract: Deploying billion-parameter Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models on industrial hardware requires fine-tuning to bridge the embodiment gap. Full Fine-Tuning (FFT) provides maximal plasticity but requires data centre-grade GPUs. We present a systematic study of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) for $π_0$, a flow-matching VLA, evaluated on four precision assembly tasks with a UR5e robotic manipulator. Across a sweep of LoRA ranks (r=8 to 256), allocation strategies, and component-freezing ablations, we find no statistically significant advantage of FFT over certain LoRA configurations. Performance saturates at r=32, and uniform allocation across the Vision-Language-Model (VLM) backbone and action expert proves sufficient. Freezing the VLM or restricting the vision encoder to LoRA significantly degrades performance, indicating that embodiment adaptation requires both semantic and visual plasticity. These results suggest that LoRA at r=32 with full vision encoder fine-tuning is a practical approach, reducing static peak VRAM from 36.2 to 10.8 GiB (parameters and optimizer states, activation memory excluded) without detectable performance loss.

</details>


### [99] [ActiveFly-Bench: Aligning Embodied Question Answering with Vision-Language-Action for Aerial Embodied Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10180)
*Weichen Zhang,Shiquan Yu,Yinan Zhu,Peizhi Tang,Shilong Ji,Zhiyuan Deng,Tianyi Lyu,Haoyang Wang,Xin Zeng,Chen Gao,Yong Li,Xinlei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActiveFly-Bench是首个连接网络空间推理与物理世界交互的无人机具身感知基准，包含三个层次化任务（Air-EQA、OBP、FLUC），并开发了闭环无人机智能体ActiveFly，实验表明现有模型在行为规划和视角调整方面仍面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机感知研究缺乏将高层任务理解、行为规划与低层控制连接起来的统一基准，亟需一个能够桥接网络空间推理与物理世界交互的评估框架。

Method: 将主动感知分解为三个层次化任务（Air-EQA、OBP、FLUC），从真实世界和仿真户外环境收集数据集，并开发了集成视觉-语言推理与细粒度控制的闭环无人机智能体ActiveFly。

Result: 当前无人机智能体在行为规划、视角调整和鲁棒任务完成方面仍存在困难，这些结果确立了ActiveFly-Bench作为具身空中智能新测试床的地位。

Conclusion: ActiveFly-Bench有效揭示了现有VLM和VLA模型在无人机主动感知中的不足，为具身空中智能提供了新的评估基准和发展方向。

Abstract: We introduce ActiveFly-Bench, the first benchmark to bridge cyberspace reasoning and physical-world interaction for UAV embodied perception. The benchmark decomposes active perception into three hierarchical tasks: Aerial Embodied Question Answering (Air-EQA), Observation Behavior Planning (OBP), and Fine-grained Language-guided UAV Control (FLUC), explicitly connecting high-level task understanding, behavior planning, and low-level control. The datasets are collected from both real-world and simulated outdoor environments for training and evaluation. We further develop ActiveFly, a closed-loop UAV agent that integrates visual-language reasoning with fine-grained control, and deploy it on a physical UAV platform. Experiments with representative VLMs and VLA models show that current UAV agents still struggle with behavior planning, viewpoint adjustment, and robust task completion in active perception. These results establish ActiveFly-Bench as a new testbed for embodied aerial intelligence.

</details>


### [100] [Source-Lifted Flow Matching for Intervenable Multimodal Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10206)
*He Zhang,Ying Sun,Pengteng Li,Ziyang Chen,Yiren Zhao,Ziyang Rao,Weiyu Guo,Yandong Guo,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SL-FM提出源可干预的流匹配策略，通过正交源提升将被动随机性转化为可控干预变量，在不分解速度场的条件下实现多模态动作控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配策略虽能建模复杂多模态动作分布，但其随机性是被动的：重复采样可生成多样行为，但用户无法在相同状态下直接选择有效的延续路径。

Method: 提出正交源提升（Orthogonal Source Lifting）机制，将手柄特定的源提升到辅助正交坐标中，目标保持在原始动作子空间；学习状态依赖的源混合分布，使用责任下限（responsibility floor）给予弱监督，防止死模式。

Result: 在交叉流诊断和机器人控制基准测试中，SL-FM将被动源随机性转化为可操作的干预变量，消除交叉引起的复合轨迹，在匹配前缀干预中91.1%改变未来路径，多个基准设置中性能提升。

Conclusion: 源几何结构无需对速度场进行模态条件化即可提供可操作的多模态控制能力。

Abstract: Flow-matching policies are promising for imitation learning because they model complex multimodal action distributions. However, their stochasticity is largely passive: repeated sampling may yield diverse behaviors, but users cannot directly choose among valid continuations from the same state. We propose Source-Lifted Flow Matching (SL-FM), a source-intervenable flow-matching policy that exposes such a handle while keeping the velocity field shared and latent-free. The handle selects only the source endpoint of the conditional flow, not a mode-specific field, preserving the standard formulation while avoiding decomposition into separate mode-conditioned dynamics. The core mechanism is \textbf{Orthogonal Source Lifting}, designed to prevent path-crossing ambiguity. Instead of partitioning target actions by mode, SL-FM lifts handle-specific sources into auxiliary orthogonal coordinates and keeps targets in the original action subspace. This preserves the demonstrated action distribution while allowing one shared field to carry different branches without merging at crossings. To keep handles usable across states, we learn a state-dependent source mixture end to end and use a responsibility floor, giving each handle weak supervision and mitigating dead modes. Experiments on crossing-flow diagnostics and robot-control benchmarks show that SL-FM converts passive source randomness into an actionable intervention variable. It removes crossing-induced composite trajectories, changes future routes in 91.1\% of matched-prefix interventions, and achieves strong free-deployment performance, with improvements in several benchmark settings. Overall, source geometry provides actionable multimodal control without conditioning the velocity field on the selected mode.

</details>


### [101] [WarpMPC: Large-Batch MPC on GPU via ADMM with Unrolled $LDL^\top$ Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11603)
*Henrik Hose,Se Hwan Jeon,Charles Khazoom,Sangbae Kim,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对GPU的大批量序列二次规划(SQP)迭代的数值优化方法，通过展开稀疏线性分解和求解、优化内存布局等策略，在非线性机器人控制任务上实现8,000-250,000次SQP迭代/秒的吞吐量，比基线快3-25倍，并能在4分钟内训练神经网络MPC近似器用于实际四旋翼飞行器控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MPC求解器在处理大批量问题时，交替方向乘子法(ADMM)求解器中的稀疏线性分解和求解过程成为计算瓶颈，存在内存访问瓶颈和计算浪费问题。

Method: 1) 利用批次内所有MPC问题实例共享相同的时域、代价和约束稀疏性这一洞察，展开稀疏线性分解和求解；2) 优化内存布局；3) 通过填充减少展开分解的分段；4) 依赖级调度的回代求解，同时加速灵敏度计算。

Result: 在非线性倒立摆、四旋翼飞行器和人形机器人基准测试上，吞吐量达到8,000-250,000次SQP迭代/秒，比基线快3-25倍；可在4分钟内合成数据集并训练神经网络MPC近似器，在硬件实验中成功稳定纳秒级四旋翼飞行器。

Conclusion: WarpMPC工具箱通过针对GPU的大批量SQP迭代数值优化，显著提升了MPC求解效率，并展示了在机器人控制中的实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper introduces numerical optimizations for maximizing throughput on GPU when solving large batches (10,000 to over 100,000) of sequential quadratic programming (SQP) iterations, where all problems have the same structure. The optimizations are implemented in a toolbox WarpMPC for model-predictive control (MPC) in JAX and Warp. Based on the insight that all MPC problem instances in a batch share the same sparsity in time, cost, and constraints, we propose unrolling sparse linear factorizations and solves, which dominate alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) solver runtime. We avoid memory access bottlenecks and wasting computations via optimized memory layout, padding-reducing segmentation of the unrolled factorization, and dependency level scheduled backsolves, additionally accelerating sensitivity computation. We achieve throughputs of 8,000 to 250,000 SQP iterations per second on nonlinear cartpole, quadrotor, and humanoid robot benchmarks, outperforming baselines by 3$\times$ to 25$\times$. We illustrate practical usefulness by synthesizing a dataset and training a neural network approximation of an MPC in under 4 minutes that stabilizes a nano quadrotor in hardware experiments.

</details>


### [102] [Coordinated Incremental Trajectory Tracking of a Tailsitter Drone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11651)
*Evangelos Ntouros,Ewoud J. J. Smeur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对尾座式无人机在协调飞行条件下，利用简化气动模型推导出解析微分平坦变换，采用旋转矩阵公式避免了欧拉角的歧义，将现有微分平坦控制器扩展到具有显著垂直速度分量的飞行状态，并通过实验验证了其优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于微分平坦的控制器在处理显著垂直速度分量的飞行状态时失效，因此需要一种新的微分平坦变换方法来扩展其适用范围。

Method: 使用简化气动模型，在协调飞行条件下推导解析微分平坦变换，采用纯旋转矩阵公式以避免欧拉角歧义。

Result: 通过实验验证，该框架在涉及显著垂直速度分量的轨迹中展示了优势。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将现有微分平坦控制器的适用性扩展到具有大垂直速度分量的飞行状态，为尾座式无人机在更广泛飞行场景中的控制提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This paper derives an analytical differential flatness transform for a tailsitter Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) under coordinated flight conditions using a simplified aerodynamic model. The proposed framework is formulated exclusively using rotation matrices, avoiding the ambiguities inherent to Euler angle representations. The method extends the applicability of an existing state-of-the-art differential flatness-based controller to flight regimes involving a significant vertical velocity component, where the previous approach becomes inapplicable. The proposed framework is validated experimentally with trajectories that highlight its advantages in these regimes.

</details>


### [103] [PIER-Flow: Physics-Informed Efficient Rectified Flow for Real-Time Mobile Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10288)
*Shibo Li,Zhongcheng Wang,Jiahe Cao,Jianhua Yang,Ke Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PIER-Flow，一种基于整流流（Rectified Flow）的轻量级导航策略，通过蒸馏MPC专家知识为连续时间ODE，实现单步动作生成（~1.29ms），在密集动态环境中达到98.85%成功率和零碰撞，并在资源受限的边缘设备上稳定运行（~5.3ms）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，基于优化的MPC虽能处理运动学和安全性约束但实时性不足，确定性行为克隆策略缺乏多模态表达能力，扩散策略虽能捕捉多模态但迭代去噪耗时。需要一种兼具物理可行性、低延迟和鲁棒性的轻量级导航策略。

Method: 将MPC专家知识蒸馏为连续时间ODE（整流流），通过并行潜在采样和轻量级可行性选择实现单步动作生成；引入物理信息训练目标强制运动学一致性；采用异步动作分块架构支持仿真到真实世界的鲁棒部署。

Result: 仿真中达到98.85%成功率和零碰撞，平均推理延迟~1.29ms，比MPC快37.2倍，比标准扩散模型快800倍以上；在资源受限边缘设备上推理延迟稳定在~5.3ms，无延迟尖峰和冻结事件。

Conclusion: PIER-Flow通过将MPC蒸馏为单步ODE高效解决了密集动态环境中的自主导航问题，在速度、成功率和部署鲁棒性上显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in dense and highly dynamic environments requires both physically feasible control and low-latency replanning. Optimization-based methods such as Model Predictive Control (MPC) explicitly handle robot kinematics and safety constraints, but repeated nonlinear optimization can limit real-time responsiveness. Deterministic behavior-cloning policies enable efficient inference but may fail to represent multimodal avoidance behaviors, whereas diffusion policies capture multimodality at the cost of time-consuming iterative denoising. We propose PIER-Flow (Physics-Informed Efficient Rectified Flow), a lightweight navigation policy for mobile robots. By distilling an MPC expert into a continuous-time Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE), PIER-Flow achieves single-step action generation through parallel latent sampling and lightweight feasibility selection. We introduce a physics-informed training objective to enforce kinematic consistency, paired with an asynchronous action chunking architecture for robust sim-to-real deployment. Extensive simulations demonstrate that PIER-Flow achieves a 98.85\% success rate and zero collisions, with an average inference of $\sim$1.29 ms, which accelerates planning by 37.2$\times$ compared to MPC and over 800$\times$ against standard diffusion models. Crucially, real-world deployment on a resource-constrained edge computer further achieves an approximately stable inference latency of $\sim$5.3 ms, avoiding the latency spikes and freezing events observed with planning baselines.

</details>


### [104] [MIRA: A Modular Open-Source Micro-UAV for Indoor Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11785)
*Lucas K. de Oliveira,Felipe A. G. Tommaselli,João Aires Marsicano,Marco S. Tayar,Pedro A. R. Saraiva,Ricardo V. Godoy,Marcelo Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MIRA是一款低成本、开源、基于3D打印的模块化室内微型无人机，通过容器化软件和Micro XRCE-DDS通信桥接，实现了子系统可替换的白盒架构，实验验证其通信延迟中位数0.02ms，结构振动集中在90-110Hz频段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有商用微型无人机难以提供底层修改权限，而自制平台需要大量工程投入，限制了研究范围。MIRA旨在提供一个低成本、易修改、可复现的开源微型无人机平台。

Method: 采用3D打印PLA机架、容器化底层软件包、Micro XRCE-DDS通信桥接，设计为白盒架构使核心子系统可单独替换。

Result: 在光学动作捕捉环境下手动飞行测试中，通信管道中位延迟为0.02ms；功率谱密度分析显示结构振动能量集中在90-110Hz窄带内，与低于20Hz的控制带宽隔离，且处于飞控安全阈值内。

Conclusion: MIRA成功实现了一种低成本、开源、模块化的室内微型无人机方案，有效降低了研究门槛，同时保证了通信性能和结构稳定性。

Abstract: Indoor robotics research increasingly relies on micro-UAVs whose airframe, electronics, and control software are fully open to modification. Off-the-shelf platforms rarely expose the low-level access required for such modifications, while building a custom alternative typically requires substantial engineering effort before flight testing can begin, leaving many laboratories to work within constraints that limit the scope of their research. We present MIRA (Modular Indoor Research Architecture), a low-cost, open-source micro-UAV for indoor research built around a replicable 3D-printed PLA airframe and a containerized low-level software package managing the companion-to-autopilot communication bridge via Micro XRCE-DDS. Designed as a white-box architecture, core subsystems are individually replaceable without firmware refactoring, supporting local fabrication and component substitution from existing lab inventory. We characterize MIRA through manual flight in position-control mode within an optical motion-capture volume, where the communication pipeline sustains a median companion-to-autopilot latency of 0.02 ms and power spectral density analysis confirms the structural vibration energy stays concentrated in a narrow 90 to 110 Hz band, isolated from the sub-20 Hz control bandwidth and within the autopilot safety thresholds.

</details>


### [105] [Soft Eversion Robots for Colonoscopy: Challenges, Open Problems, and Emerging Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10294)
*Cem Suulker,Thomas Mack,Qingzheng Cong,Neri Niccolò Dei,Reza Kashef,Mohammad Sheikh Sofla,Kaspar Althoefer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对四种新型外翻式结肠镜机器人架构进行了基准测试，分析了它们在满足结肠镜检查临床需求（长度、直径、弯曲角度、工作通道）方面的权衡，重点指出了在保持软体、低摩擦特性的同时集成转向和有效载荷输送的难题，并提供了材料选择、转向策略和临床转化的设计指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统结肠镜检查存在患者不适和操作风险，促使研究者探索以压力驱动尖端生长方式推进的外翻式机器人，这类机器人消除了与结肠壁的滑动摩擦，有望实现更低侵入性的肠道遍历。

Method: 对四种最新外翻式机器人架构进行基准测试，对比它们满足结肠长度、最小管腔直径、弯曲角度和工作通道等关键解剖与临床约束的能力，并分析各设计中的核心权衡关系。

Result: 识别出各设计中的核心权衡，特别是在集成转向和有效载荷输送时难以保持软体、低摩擦特性这一关键矛盾。

Conclusion: 提供了材料选择、转向策略和有效载荷输送方面的设计指导，并指出了临床转化中待解决的开放性问题。

Abstract: Conventional colonoscopy remains limited by patient discomfort and procedural risks, motivating research into compliant robotic alternatives. Eversion robots, which advance via pressure-driven tip growth, eliminate sliding friction against the colon wall and offer a less intrusive approach to traversal. However, no existing design simultaneously satisfies all clinical requirements. This paper benchmarks four recent eversion robot architectures against the key anatomical and clinical constraints of colonoscopy, including colon length, minimum luminal diameter, bending angle, and working-channel needs. We identify the central trade-offs each design reveals, particularly the difficulty of integrating steering and payload delivery without sacrificing the soft, low-friction behaviour that makes eversion robots attractive. We then provide design guidance across material selection, steering strategy, and payload delivery, and highlight open problems for clinical translation.

</details>


### [106] [PrismAD: Decoupled Planning via Semantic Mixture-of-Planners for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10336)
*Kang Ding,Zhigui Lin,Hongsong Wang,Jie Gui,Qi Liu,Zhe Wang,Luqi Tang,Lei He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PrismAD提出了一种基于语义混合规划器的解耦端到端自动驾驶框架，将场景token划分为交互、几何和意图三组，由独立专家处理，并通过语义感知路由器自适应聚合预测结果，在nuScenes和NeuroNCAP上取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规划器将异构场景token聚合到耦合表示空间，迫使单一规划分支同时建模智能体交互、道路几何和驾驶意图，这种耦合削弱了因子特定推理能力，并模糊了不同规划线索的贡献。

Method: PrismAD将场景token划分为交互、几何和意图三组，分配给具有相同架构但独立参数的专家；每个专家学习专门的运动规划表示；语义感知路由器为运动预测和自车规划分别分配路由权重；引入稀疏Top-K激活和噪声门控提升鲁棒性并减少计算开销。

Result: 在nuScenes开放循环数据集和NeuroNCAP闭环基准上的大量实验表明PrismAD具有竞争性表现。

Conclusion: 解耦的专家混合架构能有效改善端到端自动驾驶中不同规划因子的独立推理能力，同时保持整体性能竞争力。

Abstract: This letter presents PrismAD, a decoupled end-to-end autonomous driving framework based on a Semantic Mixture-of-Planners. Existing planners usually aggregate heterogeneous scene tokens into a coupled representation space, forcing a single planning branch to jointly model agent interaction, road geometry, and driving intention. Such coupling may weaken factor-specific reasoning and obscure the contribution of different planning cues. To address this limitation, PrismAD partitions scene tokens into interaction, geometry, and intent groups, and assigns them to independent planning experts with the same architecture but separate parameters. Each expert learns a specialized motion-planning representation, while a semantics-aware router adaptively aggregates expert predictions with separate routing weights for motion prediction and ego planning. Sparse top-$K$ activation with noisy gating is further introduced to improve routing robustness and reduce unnecessary expert computation. Extensive experiments on the nuScenes open-loop dataset and NeuroNCAP closed-loop benchmark demonstrate that PrismAD exhibits competitive performance. Our code will be released soon.

</details>


### [107] [VINE: Taming Generative Control Policies for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10369)
*Rushuai Yang,Zhuo Han,Houlin Li,Hecheng Wang,Zhichao Wu,Rui Zhang,Zhaowei Zhang,Zihong Chen,Xiaohan Yan,Chiming Liu,Yi Chen,Wei Shan,Maoqing Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现流匹配策略在价值梯度强化学习中的不稳定性并非源于迭代生成本身，而是源于原本为行为克隆设计的采样策略。为此提出VINE方法，通过在每个去噪步骤重构新的插值状态，实现稳定的端到端价值梯度优化，在保留流匹配表达力和迭代生成能力的同时，在OGBench离线RL基准和真实机器人操作任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配策略在机器人学习中表现出色，但将其与价值梯度强化学习结合时存在训练不稳定性问题。已有方法将不稳定性归因于迭代生成过程，因此通过牺牲迭代生成、高表达力或价值梯度优化来避免端到端优化。本文旨在揭示不稳定的真正根源并提出解决方案。

Method: 提出VINE（RL导向的采样方法）：在每个去噪步骤中，不沿单一流轨迹前进，而是重构新的插值状态，从而创建稳定的可微路径用于价值梯度传播，同时保持与原始流匹配去噪过程的兼容性。

Result: VINE在所有十个去噪步骤中执行端到端反向传播，实现了稳定的策略改进，在OGBench离线RL基准和真实机器人操作任务上持续超越最先进的RL方法。

Conclusion: 流匹配策略在价值梯度RL中的不稳定性源于vanilla采样策略而非迭代生成本身；VINE方法在不牺牲迭代生成、表达力和端到端价值梯度优化的前提下，实现了稳定且性能优越的策略学习。

Abstract: Flow-matching policies have emerged as an effective policy parameterization for robot learning. They iteratively generate actions from noise, enabling highly expressive modeling of complex and multimodal action distributions. However, prior works observed that scaling these policies with value-gradient reinforcement learning (RL) often leads to training instability. Existing methods attribute this instability to iterative generation and therefore avoid end-to-end value-gradient optimization by sacrificing iterative generation, high expressiveness, or value-gradient optimization. Contrary to prior belief, we show the instability does not stem from iterative generation itself, but from the vanilla sampling strategy originally designed for behavior cloning, which becomes brittle under value-gradient RL. Motivated by this insight, we propose VINE, an RL-oriented sampling method that enables stable end-to-end value-gradient optimization for flow-matching policies. Instead of following a single flow trajectory, VINE reconstructs a new interpolation state at every denoising step, creating a stable differentiable path for value-gradient propagation while remaining compatible with the original flow-matching denoising process. As a result, VINE preserves the expressiveness and iterative generation of flow-matching without sacrificing end-to-end value-gradient optimization. Despite performing end-to-end backpropagation through all ten denoising steps, VINE achieves stable policy improvement and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art RL methods on the OGBench offline RL benchmark and real-world robotic manipulation task. Videos are available on our website: https://agibottech.github.io/vine.

</details>


### [108] [Robotic Contextual Awareness for Human-Robot Collaboration and Environmental Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10372)
*Federico Rollo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文研究了自主移动机器人从工业环境向动态人本环境过渡中的安全自主运行问题，提出了两个互补研究方向：人类重识别与跟踪以改善人机协作，以及增强机器人对环境的几何与语义理解能力，从而提升机器人的情境感知，实现更安全、自然的人机交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主移动机器人正从受控工业环境转向动态、以人为中心的环境（如制造业、物流、医疗），但其缺乏全面的情境感知能力，无法识别空间环境中的物体和参与者，导致难以规划自适应行为或与人类进行有意义的交互。

Method: 论文探索了两个互补研究方向：1) 人类重识别与跟踪方法，使移动机器人能够识别特定个体以实现针对性协作；2) 增强机器人综合感知能力，从几何（运动规划与碰撞避免）和语义（更丰富的交互理解）两个层面理解环境。

Result: 两种方案均提升了机器人的语义理解能力，增强了对周围环境的认知，从而实现更流畅自然的人-机-环境交互。

Conclusion: 本工作在人类重识别和环境理解方面的贡献，标志着向更具情境感知能力的机器人迈出了重要一步，有望实现更安全的人机共存和更有效的人机协作。

Abstract: The transition of autonomous mobile robots from controlled industrial settings to dynamic, human-centric environments, such as manufacturing, logistics, and healthcare, has made their safe and autonomous operation a critical area of research. These sophisticated machines must be capable of perceiving, understanding, and interacting with their surroundings to navigate freely and perform complex tasks. A significant obstacle to achieving this is the lack of comprehensive contextual awareness, which requires a robot to recognize its spatial environment and identify the objects and actors within it. Without this perceptual knowledge, robots struggle to plan adaptive behaviors or engage in meaningful interaction with humans.
  This thesis presents novel solutions to this challenge by exploring two distinct but complementary research directions. The first direction involves human re-identification and tracking to improve Human-Robot Collaboration. Our developed approach enables a mobile robot to recognize a specific person, facilitating targeted collaboration while ignoring other individuals. The second direction focuses on enhancing the robot's overall perceptual capabilities to understand its environment geometrically and semantically. Geometric information is vital for motion planning and collision avoidance, while semantic knowledge provides the robot with a richer understanding for more advanced interaction. Both solutions are driven by the improvement of the semantical understanding of robots that enhance their knowledge of their surroundings, allowing a smoother and more natural interaction between robots, humans, and the environment. The contributions of this work in human re-identification and environmental understanding represent a significant step toward a future where robots are more contextually aware, enabling safer coexistence and more effective collaboration.

</details>


### [109] [Real-Time Rulebook-Aware Nonlinear MPC for Autonomous Driving with Priority-Biased Tiered Slacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10975)
*Hadi Hajieghrary,Benedikt Walter,Chaitanya Shinde,Paul Schmitt,Miguel Hurtado*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: W-SQP是一种加权分层松弛非线性模型预测控制器，将九种驾驶规则族编译为四层共享松弛非线性规划，通过强分离的层级惩罚实现优先级偏置的实时轨迹优化，在150个Waymo场景中验证了其可审计性和实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主车辆运动规划器需要在实时性约束下解决安全、法规、舒适性和效率之间的冲突，同时使决策过程可审计。

Method: 提出W-SQP框架：1) 将九种驾驶规则族组织为四层优先级共享松弛项；2) 采用加权二次松弛惩罚实现强分离的层级间违反偏置；3) 利用CasADi和IPOPT在线求解非线性规划；4) 以10Hz频率重规划，设定90ms求解时限返回任意迭代解并投影至车辆动力学。

Result: 中位和最大求解时间分别为28ms和104ms；在150个Waymo场景中，W-SQP在与专家回放比较时未表现出系统性的组级安全与合规缺陷，仅在难度最高、与人类轨迹分歧最大的场景中出现局部退化。

Conclusion: W-SQP被定性为一个可审计、优先级偏置、具备任意时间能力的NMPC原型，而非硬实时或形式化安全的控制器。

Abstract: Autonomous-vehicle motion planners must resolve conflicts among safety, regulation, comfort, and efficiency in real time while exposing those decisions for audit. We present W-SQP, a weighted tiered-slack nonlinear model predictive controller (NMPC) that compiles nine driving-rule families into a four-tier shared-slack nonlinear program solved online with CasADi and IPOPT; the name denotes the weighted quadratic slack penalty, not a sequential-quadratic-programming solver. Strongly separated tier penalties bias residual violations toward lower-priority rules while leaving actuation bounds hard. The controller replans from its executed state at $10$\,Hz and records per-rule residuals on every cycle. A $90$\,ms solver-time limit returns an anytime iterate that is projected through the vehicle dynamics before execution; median and maximum observed wall-clock solve times were $28$ and $104$\,ms.
